实验效果: 受控消融显示训练在噪声层级上的分配比动作如何进入网络更重要；在对照真实执行的 WorldOracle 基准上，Dianjing 在五个评测赛道中的四个取得最佳成绩，在 AgiBot-Long 上排名第二。

Abstract: Modern video generators excel at producing visually plausible futures guided by text and image prompts. Yet their emphasis on semantic plausibility does not guarantee fidelity to the physical consequences of specific actions. Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs), in contrast, aim to simulate how the world responds to robot actions, enabling scalable policy evaluation and improvement without costly real-world rollouts. The challenge is not merely to generate a plausible future, but to predict what follows from a specific physical action. As video priors grow stronger, we ask how little adaptation is truly needed to bridge this gap. We introduce Dianjing, a minimal recipe that preserves and unlocks the capabilities of strong video priors through unified physical action conditioning, training concentrated at the high noise levels where motion and layout, and thus the consequences of actions, are decided, and native history conditioning. Built upon MiniMax H3, a 33B video foundation model, Dianjing freezes the pretrained backbone and trains only 0.34% of its parameters using approximately 65 hours of robot video across two embodiments, without additional robot-specific pretraining or camera calibration. Controlled ablations show that where training is allocated across noise levels matters more than how actions enter the network. On WorldOracle, which evaluates predictions against paired real-world executions, Dianjing achieves the best scores on four of the five evaluation tracks and ranks second on AgiBot-Long. Our findings suggest that strong video priors already bring us most of the way to world modeling; lightweight physical action grounding can unlock their potential for real-world prediction.

</details>


### [175] [PreserveReward: Reliable Consistency Reward Modeling for Image Editing via Dual-Teacher On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34446)
*Xueheng Li,Yong Liu,Wen Xue,Shi-Yu Tian,Zhuofan Xia,Chengjun Xie,Yipeng Sun,Simiu Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PreserveReward——一种结合视觉 On-Policy 自蒸馏的一致性奖励模型，用于提升指令引导图像编辑中对未编辑内容保持性的可靠评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 指令引导图像编辑中需评估编辑后图像对应当保留内容的保持程度，该一致性评估既用于衡量内容保持，也为强化学习提供奖励信号。输入为编辑前后的图像对及编辑指令，输出为与参考保持分数对齐的一致性评分。

Motivation: 现有奖励模型可能忽略细微视觉变化，或无法区分必要修改与非预期改动，导致一致性评估的可靠性不足，难以支撑内容保持度量与 RL 奖励。

Method: PreserveReward 以回归式评分损失从参考保持分数中学习，并引入视觉 On-Policy 自蒸馏以增强细粒度视觉感知：采用两个互补教师视图，局部教师用匹配裁剪块捕捉细微变化，联合教师融合局部细节与全局上下文判断变化是否符合编辑意图；同时通过最小 KL 投影在参考分数误差约束下校准教师分数分布，并依据与评分目标的梯度对齐自适应选择教师过程监督，以缓解评分偏差与优化冲突。

实验效果: 在多个基准上的实验表明，PreserveReward 在非编辑内容保持方面与人类偏好高度对齐，并在多样化编辑场景中具有良好泛化能力（摘要未报告具体数值）。

Abstract: Instruction-guided image editing has a wide range of applications, yet existing models may inadvertently alter content that should remain unchanged. Reliable consistency evaluation is therefore essential for assessing content preservation and providing reward signals for Reinforcement Learning (RL). However, existing reward models may overlook subtle visual changes or fail to distinguish necessary modifications from unintended alterations, limiting the reliability of consistency assessment. To address this challenge, we propose PreserveReward, a consistency reward model for image editing that integrates visual On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) to enhance fine-grained visual perception and consistency assessment. PreserveReward learns from reference preservation scores through a regression-based scoring loss and employs two complementary teacher views for self-distillation. The local teacher uses matched crops to identify subtle visual changes, while the joint teacher combines local details with global context to determine whether these changes align with the editing intent. To improve the reliability of distillation supervision, we calibrate teacher score distributions via minimum-KL projection under reference-score error constraints and adaptively select teacher process supervision based on gradient alignment with the scoring objective, mitigating scoring bias and optimization conflicts. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that PreserveReward achieves strong alignment with human preference on non-edited content preservation and generalizes well across diverse editing scenarios.

</details>


### [176] [ProxyGenBench: Benchmarking Proxy-Guided Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34457)
*Zhenchen Tang,Haolin Lu,Songlin Yang,Xiaotong Zhao,Ran Zhang,Bo Peng,Shuai Li,Haotian Fan,Haobin Zhong,Yanni Zhang,Songlian Li,Ning Zhang,Xun Gao,Alan Zhao,Jing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 ProxyGenBench 基准，用于系统评测以动画3D粗模为结构代理的视频生成在保持结构、补全内容与多模态绑定三方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是由动画3D blockout（结构代理）引导视频生成，代理规定场景布局、运动、相机行为与事件顺序，同时可结合文本、图像、音频条件，目标是生成既符合预设结构又细节合理的真实视频。

Motivation: 现有视频生成虽能生成真实视频，但可控性不足；结构代理输入粗糙、细节未指定且自身带有不应被复现的伪影，因此需要研究"保持结构—补全缺失—绑定条件"这三项相互作用的评测需求，而此前缺乏针对性基准。

Method: 构建 ProxyGenBench：涵盖来自60个场景、3类场景任务的312个评测案例，并对代理保真度、文本详细程度和多模态输入进行受控变化；采用经核验的、逐案例定制清单对5种视频生成器进行评测，从而分别考察结构保持、内容补全与伪影纠正、以及实体—事件绑定。

实验效果: 实验表明代理能提升时空可控性，但代理细节越多反而可能伴随更差的补全效果，且强结构遵循并不保证正确的条件—实体绑定。

Abstract: Recent video generators produce realistic videos, yet controlling how scenes unfold remains challenging. Structural proxies, such as animated 3D blockouts, guide generation by specifying scene layout, motion, camera behavior, and event order. However, these coarse inputs leave details unspecified and may contain artifacts that should not be reproduced. Successful proxy-guided generation must therefore Preserve intended structure, Complete missing content and correct proxy artifacts, and Bind text, image, and audio conditions to the right entities and events. We introduce ProxyGenBench to study these interacting requirements, with 312 evaluation cases from 60 scenes across three scene tasks and controlled variations in proxy fidelity, text detail, and multimodal inputs. We evaluate five video generators using verified, case-specific checklists. Experiments show that proxies improve spatiotemporal control, but greater proxy detail can coincide with poorer completion, while strong structural adherence does not ensure correct binding.

</details>


### [177] [SceneDesigner++: A Unified 9-DoF Pose Control Framework for T2I and T2V Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34461)
*Xincheng Shuai,Zhenyuan Qin,Henghui Ding,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneDesigner++ 是一个在 T2I 与 T2V 生成中统一实现 9-DoF 物体位姿控制的框架，通过解耦的绝对与相对位姿编码提升位姿保真度与时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是让用户在 3D 空间中精确指定物体的位置、尺寸与朝向（9-DoF），用于视觉内容生成；输入为文本提示与目标位姿，输出为符合该位姿的图像或视频，目标是同时获得高精度位姿可控性与时序连贯性。

Motivation: 现有方法通常只针对 T2I 或 T2V 单一路径，且在位姿可控性与时序一致性上均有不足，缺少跨两种生成任务的统一 9-DoF 位姿控制方案，也缺少带精确位姿标注的数据与评测基准。

Method: 构建 ObjectPose9D++ 数据集（含精确位姿标注的图像与视频）；绝对位姿编码利用 CNOCS 图序列提供逐帧显式 9-DoF 引导，相对位姿编码 VPRoPE 将相对位姿注入旋转位置编码以建模帧间位姿转移；为每个注意力头引入可学习正交变换，使不同头捕捉互补几何关系并保持特征范数以稳定训练；采用两阶段训练分别学习绝对与相对编码；该轻量解耦设计还可与独立训练的相机控制模块结合，实现物体与相机的分离或联合控制；并提出 T2IVPoseEval 基准。

实验效果: 摘要未给出具体数值，仅报告在同骨干基线上，SceneDesigner++ 在位姿保真度与运动质量上均持续更优，并在 T2I 与 T2V 任务上通过 T2IVPoseEval 基准评估。

Abstract: Precise 9-DoF pose control is essential for visual content generation, enabling users to specify object location, size, and orientation in 3D space. However, existing methods typically focus on either text-to-image (T2I) or text-to-video (T2V) generation, with limitations in pose controllability and temporal coherence. We present SceneDesigner++, a unified framework for 9-DoF pose control across both T2I and T2V generation. We construct ObjectPose9D++, a dataset containing high-quality images and videos with accurate pose annotations. Trained on ObjectPose9D++, SceneDesigner++ combines absolute and relative pose encoding strategies to decouple frame-wise spatial grounding from inter-frame motion modeling. Specifically, absolute pose encoding leverages CNOCS map sequences to provide explicit, frame-wise 9-DoF pose guidance. Meanwhile, our relative pose encoding, termed VPRoPE, incorporates relative pose information into rotary position embeddings to explicitly model inter-frame pose transitions, thereby promoting temporally coherent object motion. A learnable orthogonal transformation is further introduced for each attention head, allowing different heads to capture complementary geometric relationships while preserving input feature norms to promote training stability. We further adopt a two-stage training strategy to learn the absolute and relative pose encoding mechanisms separately. Benefiting from this lightweight and decoupled design, SceneDesigner++ also supports integration with an independently trained camera-control module, enabling separate or joint control over object and camera motion. Finally, we introduce T2IVPoseEval, a benchmark for evaluating diverse object poses and motion patterns in both T2I and T2V tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SceneDesigner++ consistently outperforms same-backbone baselines in pose fidelity and motion quality.

</details>


### [178] [Evidence-Aligned Benchmarking and Learning for Medical Video Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34465)
*Zhongpai Gao,Anwesa Choudhuri,Meng Zheng,Benjamin Planche,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MedEvidU，从基准、编码器输入与训练数据三方面强化医疗视频语言模型的视觉证据接地能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 医疗视频语言模型需依据视频中的视觉证据回答提问。本文以视频-问题为输入，目标是让模型答案真正对准被问及的帧与器械等视觉要素，而非依赖文本先验。

Motivation: 现有工作存在三处失效：基准奖励忽略视频的答案（某数据集中片段在录制中的位置即可预测手术步骤，占37%的题目；常数答案在标准安全评估指标上优于所有训练模型）；常用视频编码器将连续帧合并为共享 token，导致单帧问题由两帧混合信息回答；微调模型已记忆训练问题，可学信息有限。

Method: 分三步：构建 MedVidBench-v2（来自18个手术与临床视频数据集的34项任务，提示与指标经“无视频作答”审计，并测试未见医院与从整段录制中盲切的时间窗）；采用 Query-slot 输入，为被查询帧分配独立 token，几乎不增加开销；提出标签匹配问题重采样（LQR），在最后一个监督 epoch 及强化学习中引入关于同一录制的未见问题并保持答案分布不变。

实验效果: 4B 模型接地 IoU 由 30.4 提升至 44.4，超过监督训练的 27B 模型；未见医院的器械接地由 33.9 提升至 72.5；盲切窗口上事件存在准确率提升5.5点，定位 F1 提升8.1点。

Abstract: A medical video language model should answer from the visual evidence its question asks about. We find three places where this breaks down and address each with an approach we call MedEvidU. First, benchmarks reward answers that ignore the video: on one dataset, the position of a clip within its recording predicts the surgical step in 37% of questions, and a constant answer beats every trained model on a standard safety-assessment metric. We build MedVidBench-v2, 34 tasks from 18 surgical and clinical video datasets, in which prompts and metrics are audited with answers given without video, and which tests unseen hospitals and windows cut blindly from whole recordings. Second, common video encoders merge consecutive frames into shared tokens, so a question about one frame is answered from a blend of two. Query-slot inputs give each queried frame its own tokens at almost no extra cost. Third, a fine-tuned model has memorized its training questions, which leaves little to learn from. Label-matched question resampling (LQR) adds unseen questions about the same recordings while keeping the answer distribution fixed, in a final supervised epoch and in reinforcement learning. Together, these raise the grounding IoU of a 4B model from 30.4 to 44.4, above a supervised 27B model, and instrument grounding at an unseen hospital from 33.9 to 72.5. On blindly cut windows, event-presence accuracy improves by 5.5 points and localization F1 by 8.1 points. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/MedEvidU/

</details>


### [179] [Attacking Hard-Label Large Vision-Language Models with Model-Sensitive Adversarial Patch Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34467)
*Nian Ai,Guangke Chen,Xiaowen Cai,Zhongliang Guo,Daizong Liu,Pan Zhou,Ognjen Arandjelović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 HardPatch，一种仅通过查询黑盒模型、在 hard-label 设定下生成视觉对抗补丁来攻击大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是研究 LVLMs 在多模态下游任务中的对抗脆弱性：输入为图像及其分割补丁，输出为使模型满足对抗条件的扰动图像，目标是攻击现实场景中的 LVLM 应用。

Motivation: 现有攻击方法依赖从 LVLM 反向传播得到的梯度来优化对抗扰动，而现实中的 LVLM 应用不提供梯度或模型细节，导致这类方法难以扩展；这一差距促使作者转向仅需查询的 hard-label 攻击。

Method: HardPatch 先将图像均匀切分为补丁并逐一掩码，独立评估各补丁对模型的敏感度；再按敏感度降序迭代选取最脆弱补丁，用噪声初始化并用附加随机噪声估计梯度进行优化；如此逐块扰动，直至修改后的图像满足对抗条件。

实验效果: 摘要称在多个 LVLM 模型与数据集上进行了评估，表明 HardPatch 具有对抗性，但未报告具体基准数值或对比结果；仅给出定性经验观察：适当的补丁替换与优化可使 HardPatch 生成有效对抗图像以攻击 hard-label LVLM。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in handling multi-modal downstream tasks, gaining increasing popularity. However, recent studies show that LVLMs are susceptible to both intentional and inadvertent attacks. Existing attackers ideally optimize adversarial perturbations with backpropagated gradients from LVLMs, thus limiting their scalability in practical scenarios as real-world LVLM applications will not provide any LVLM's gradient or details. Motivated by this research gap and counter-practical phenomenon, we propose a novel hard-label attack method for LVLMs, named HardPatch, to generate visual adversarial patches by solely querying the model. Our method provides deeper insights into how to investigate the vulnerability of LVLMs in local visual regions and generate corresponding adversarial substitution under the practical yet challenging hard-label setting. Specifically, we first split each image into uniform patches and mask each of them to individually assess their sensitivity to the LVLM model. Then, according to the descending order of sensitive scores, we iteratively select the most vulnerable patch to initialize noise and estimate gradients with further additive random noises for optimization. In this manner, multiple patches are perturbed until the altered image satisfies the adversarial condition. Extensive LVLM models and datasets are evaluated to demonstrate the adversarial nature of HardPatch. Our empirical observations suggest that with appropriate patch substitution and optimization, HardPatch can craft effective adversarial images to attack hard-label LVLMs.

</details>


### [180] [TACC: Target-Aware Carrier Compression for Dense-Context Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34476)
*Yiming Fan,Yijia Guo,Zewen Guo,Liwen Hu,Yuheng Yuan,Yang Li,Zhiwei Li,Xitong Ling,Caihong Yuan,Shengbo Chen,Tiejun Huang,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TACC 是一个面向前馈隐式新视角合成的目标感知压缩模块，通过基于源-目标几何相关性的渐进式 token 检索，大幅降低密集输入下的内存与延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 前馈式新视角合成方法摒弃逐场景优化，直接从输入视图推断 3D 表示，以源视图为输入、目标视图渲染为输出。其目标是降低对全局场景 token 上下文的依赖，从而高效支持日益密集的源视图输入。

Motivation: 现有前馈方法依赖全局场景 token 上下文，内存与延迟开销大，难以扩展至密集源视图；而通用压缩方法的压缩策略固定且与目标无关，无法针对具体目标视图调整证据分配，不适配新视角合成任务。

Method: TACC 将每个源视图组织为渐进式载体层级（progressive carrier hierarchy），依据源-目标几何相关性自适应检索 token，动态分配精确的计算配额，从而直接得到紧凑的、以目标视图为条件的表示。

实验效果: 在 RE10K 密集输入设置下，TACC 在 LagerNVS 与 LVSM 上分别取得比 Full Context 高 9.23 倍和 64 倍的有效 FPS（Effective FPS，不含一次性源编码与载体构建开销）。

Abstract: Recent feed-forward approaches mark a paradigm shift in novel view synthesis by eliminating the reliance on per-scene optimization, directly inferring 3D representations from input views. However, their reliance on global scene-token context incurs substantial memory and latency overhead, constraining their ability to handle increasingly dense source-view contexts. Compressing source representations offers an intuitive way to limit this token budget. Prior general-purpose compression methods are not tailored for novel view synthesis, as their fixed, target-agnostic nature fails to adapt evidence allocation to individual target views. To overcome this, we introduce TACC, a target-aware compression module tailored for feed-forward latent NVS. Specifically, TACC organizes each source view into a progressive carrier hierarchy. Driven by source-target geometric relevance, it adaptively retrieves tokens to dynamically allocate exact computational quotas. This selective retrieval directly yields a compact, target-conditioned representation. Extensive experiments on the RE10K demonstrate that under dense input settings, TACC achieves $9.23\times$ and $64\times$ higher Effective FPS than Full Context on LagerNVS and LVSM, respectively. Effective FPS excludes one-time source encoding and Carrier construction.

</details>


### [181] [Feed-Forward Adaptive Gaussian Splatting for Interactive Medical Volume Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34478)
*Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Meng Zheng,Anwesa Choudhuri,Chaoyi Zhou,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RenderFM-Next 是一个面向 CT 与 MR 体数据的统一前馈高斯泼溅模型，通过一次性预测高斯、按 TF 无关的精确裁剪与自适应 1.5 mm 细化，实现高质量且 4-6 ms 级的路径追踪风格体渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务旨在用前馈模型从 CT/MR 体数据直接预测高斯，从而在改变视角、裁剪面或传输函数（TF）时无需重新拟合即可快速渲染出具有真实光照的路径追踪级图像，目标是兼顾画质与交互帧率。

Motivation: 路径追踪渲染每次改变视角、裁剪面或 TF 都要重启，帧率受限；RenderFM 虽为快速前馈方案，但仅支持 CT、每次 TF 编辑都需重跑网络、缺少裁剪算子，且高斯固定置于 3 mm 均匀网格导致器官边界与切面模糊。

Method: 用一个网络统一处理 CT 与 MR，每个体数据只预测一次高斯，使 TF 编辑无需前向推理；裁剪面借用已有算子对高斯进行精确切割。模型先预测全体的 3 mm 粗高斯，再仅在能提升图像处细化至 1.5 mm：小型打分头按“细化可降低的渲染误差”对粗高斯排序，渲染时由用户选择细化数量，且裁剪面附近的高斯强制细化以锐化切面。

实验效果: 在约 2,000 例含路径追踪参考的 CT/MR 基准上，较在同基准重训的 RenderFM 分别提升 2.8 dB（CT）与 2.4 dB（MR），1600x1600 视图渲染耗时 4-6 ms；细化相对无细化版本提升 0.30/0.69 dB（切面 0.51/1.17 dB），细化学习选出的 10% 粗高斯即可匹配随机 50% 的效果且高斯数减少 2.4 倍。

Abstract: Path-traced rendering shows CT and MR volumes with realistic lighting, but it restarts after every change of view, clipping plane or transfer function (TF), the map from voxel intensity to colour and opacity, which limits the frame rate. Feed-forward Gaussian splatting models such as RenderFM predict the Gaussians directly from the volume instead of fitting them to each scan, and are fast. RenderFM, however, handles only CT, runs its network again after every TF edit, has no clipping operator, and places its Gaussians on a uniform 3-mm grid that blurs organ boundaries and cut faces. We present RenderFM-Next, a feed-forward model that addresses these limits. One network handles both CT and MR. Its Gaussians are predicted once per volume: a new TF needs no pass of this network, and a clipping plane cuts them exactly, using operators from prior work. The model predicts coarse 3-mm Gaussians for the whole volume and refines them to 1.5 mm only where refinement improves the image. A small score head ranks the coarse Gaussians by how much refining them would reduce the rendering error; at render time the user chooses how many are refined, and those next to a clipping plane are always refined, which sharpens cut faces. We build a benchmark of about 2,000 CT and MR scans with path-traced references under clinical presets, TF edits and clipping. Our RenderFM-Next improves over RenderFM retrained on this benchmark by 2.8 dB on CT and 2.4 dB on MR and renders a 1600x1600 view in 4-6 ms. Refinement adds 0.30 / 0.69 dB over the same model trained without it (0.51 / 1.17 dB on cut faces), and refining a learned 10% of the coarse Gaussians matches a random 50% with 2.4x fewer Gaussians. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/renderfmnext/

</details>


### [182] [ProManO: Beyond Sim(3) for Metric Upgrade of Feed-Forward Geometry with Visual-Inertial Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34480)
*Lingxiang Hu,Zhenjun Zhao,Qi Shao,Naima Ait Oufroukh,Fabien Bonardi,Raymond Ghandour,Shenghai Yuan,Javier Civera,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文证明与预标定内参兼容的变换构成十维流形，并提出 ProManO 概率流形优化框架估计超越 Sim(3) 的 3 个自由度，从而用稀疏 VIO 锚点校正稠密前馈几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是将前馈（feed-forward）稠密几何预测升级为度量尺度，即用稀疏的视觉惯性里程计（VIO）锚点对齐稠密深度/几何预测；目标是找到一种既保持预标定内参不变、又能刻画深度相关畸变的正确校正变换。

Motivation: 现有方法中，相似变换（Sim(3)）无法表达依赖深度的畸变，而投影变换会破坏预标定内参，二者均不理想；论文进一步指出在 Sim(3) 之外还存在 3 个在图像中不可见、且会使残差随深度范围二次增长的自由度，只能借助沿视线方向的度量深度或多视图约束求解，现有工作未处理该问题。

Method: 作者先证明与预标定内参兼容的变换构成十维流形，并定位出超出 Sim(3) 的 3 个自由度（确定无穷远平面）。随后提出 ProManO：在单一 EM 过程中，按锚点的沿视线不确定性与内点概率对每个锚点加权，进行概率流形优化；该框架可认证相似变换同步结果，仅在可观测处精化无穷远平面，从而保证目标值不劣于最优相似对齐。

实验效果: 在 EuRoC、CID-SIMS、Oxford Spires 与 KITTI-360 四个数据集上，ProManO 取得最高平均 F1，并在全部四个数据集上优于 Sim(3) 对齐；摘要未报告具体数值。

Abstract: Upgrading feed-forward geometry to metric scale with visual-inertial odometry (VIO) raises the question of which correction should align dense feed-forward prediction with sparse metric VIO anchors. Existing methods use either a similarity transformation, which cannot represent depth-dependent distortion, or a projective transformation, which alters pre-calibrated intrinsics. We prove that the transformations compatible with pre-calibrated intrinsics form a ten-dimensional manifold. Beyond Sim(3), this manifold contains three degrees of freedom (DoF) that locate the plane at infinity and leave residuals growing quadratically with depth range after any similarity alignment. These DoF are invisible in the image and can be resolved only by metric depth along viewing rays or by multi-view constraints. To estimate them, we propose ProManO, a probabilistic manifold optimization framework in which every anchor is weighted by its along-ray uncertainty and inlier probability within a single expectation-maximization (EM) procedure. ProManO certifies the similarity synchronization and refines the plane at infinity only where it is observable, which guarantees an objective value no worse than that of the optimal similarity alignment. Experiments on EuRoC, CID-SIMS, Oxford Spires, and KITTI-360 demonstrate that ProManO achieves the highest mean F1 and outperforms Sim(3) alignment on all four datasets. The project page is available at https://h0xg.github.io/ProManO/.

</details>


### [183] [Who Is Ego? Scaling Cross-View Camera Wearer Identification and Segmentation beyond Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34489)
*Yifan Jin,Zheng Zhang,Tianwen Qian,Dong Wang,Bo Zhao,Yanwei Fu,Yuqian Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出广义相机佩戴者识别任务（FindEgo-1M 数据集）与免训练方法 EgoRay-ZS/EgoRay，利用对极几何实现佩戴者识别、分割与双向相机定位并达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 输入同步的第一人称（ego）与第三人称（exo）图像对，目标是判定 exo 图像中哪个候选是 ego 相机佩戴者（who is ego）以及其位置（where is ego）；任务从选择给定候选掩码扩展为带缺失预测的佩戴者分割与跨视角互定位，并推广到机器人、车辆、动物、无人机等多种载体。

Motivation: 现有工作仅面向人类佩戴者、依赖给定候选掩码、且通常需任务特定训练（如数据集训练的 PEN），缺乏对多载体类型、缺失情况与空间定位的统一处理。

Method: 利用几何关系：ego 相机在 exo 图像中的投影（对极点）位于佩戴者身上或附近。EgoRay-ZS 将冻结的前馈三维重建模型 VGGT-Ω 转为免训练方案，以候选掩码或分割提案到 ego 相机的角偏移与距离排序，并由相对位姿给出双向相机方向；完整版 EgoRay 在 FindEgo-1M（116 万对真实与仿真 ego-exo 数据，12 类载体、17 个域、111 个测试设置）上进行位姿监督训练，并采用以候选掩码为约束的无标签测试时自适应。

实验效果: EgoRay-ZS 在 TF2023 原始标签上取得 78.0% 识别准确率（对比数据集训练的 PEN 的 52.9%）；在 FindEgo-1M 上，EgoRay 于已见/未见设置分别达到 87.1 与 77.7 的机会校正准确率，匹配查询下较 GPT-6 Astra 分别高 31.6 与 21.3 个百分点，且运行速度快 18 倍。

Abstract: A first-person (ego) image reveals what a camera sees, but not who or what carries it. Given a synchronized third-person (exo) image, camera wearer identification asks who is ego: which candidate carries the ego camera? We extend this task beyond humans to robots, vehicles, animals, and drones, and broaden its spatial scope (where is ego) from selecting a provided candidate mask to wearer segmentation with absence prediction and mutual camera localization across ego-exo views. We introduce FindEgo-1M, comprising 1.16M real and simulated ego-exo pairs across 12 wearer types, 17 domains, and 111 test settings. Our approach exploits a geometric relation: the ego camera's projection into the exo image, i.e., the epipole, lies on or near its wearer. We introduce EgoRay-ZS, the first method to turn a frozen feed-forward 3D reconstruction model, VGGT-$Ω$, into a training-free solution for wearer identification and segmentation. Its geometric readout ranks candidate masks or segmentation proposals by angular offset and distance to the ego camera, while the relative pose provides mutual camera directions. We then develop the full EgoRay model through pose-supervised training on FindEgo-1M and label-free test-time adaptation constrained by candidate masks. Without task-specific training, EgoRay-ZS achieves 78.0% identification accuracy on TF2023's original labels, versus 52.9% reported for the dataset-trained PEN. On FindEgo-1M, EgoRay achieves state-of-the-art chance-corrected accuracy of 87.1 and 77.7 on seen and unseen settings. On matched queries, EgoRay also outperforms GPT-6 Astra by 31.6 and 21.3 points on seen and unseen settings, respectively, while running $18\times$ faster. Data, code, and models will be released.

</details>


### [184] [Angular Interpolation in Sparse-View Low-Dose CT Reconstruction: Placement Effects and Data-Consistency Step-Size Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34508)
*Yitian Tang,Yuncheng Zhang,Hualiang Wang,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统量化了角域插值在双域稀疏视图低剂量CT网络中的放置位置（正弦图初始化 vs. 图像残差基底）对性能的独立影响，并与网络结构因素区分开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务面向稀疏视图低剂量CT重建，输入为稀疏角度采样（如60视图）的投影数据及对应图像域信息，输出为高质量重建图像；目标是厘清投影域与图像域处理中插值模块的具体作用，并为网络训练步长标定提供依据。

Motivation: 已有双域网络将投影域处理与图像域处理混合使用，但插值（尤其是学习前的角度插值）究竟贡献于何处缺乏定量辨析，插值放置与网络架构的效应相互混淆，导致无法判断性能提升的真实来源。

Method: 先在合成平行束数据上评估无参数角度插值相对零填充的增益，并比较全视图FBP与插值FBP；再在数据一致性双域网络（DC-DuNet）中设计2×2配置消融，在固定分支宽度、深度与训练计划的条件下仅改变插值放置位置（正弦图初始化或插值图像基底）及其对应输入通道，并考察训练过程中的排序变化；最后在CHACOS腹部CT与独立合成数据上做免重训泛化测试，并实测投影/反投影算子的主导增益以支持NK归一化的步长标定。

实验效果: 60视图下无参数插值使无噪缺失视图MAE相对零填充降低99.7%，联合学习补全正弦图的全视图FBP较插值FBP提升1.27 dB PSNR；训练20轮后正弦图初始化带来+1.98 dB、插值图像基底带来−2.16 dB，且图像基底排序在训练中发生反转；正弦图初始化变体在60视图、10%剂量下优于纯图像U-Net 0.96 dB（独立合成数据上1.28 dB），CHACOS上免重训仍有0.12 dB优势而图像基底差异趋零；实测投影/反投影对经验主导增益约为0.85NK。

Abstract: Dual-domain networks combine projection and image processing for sparse-view low-dose computed tomography (CT). We quantify the contribution of angular interpolation before learning and distinguish its roles in sinogram initialisation and the image residual base. On synthetic parallel-beam data, parameter-free interpolation reduces noise-free missing-view mean absolute error by 99.7% relative to zero filling at 60 views. Full-view filtered back-projection (FBP) of the jointly learned completed sinogram adds 1.27 dB of peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) over interpolated FBP. In a data-consistency dual-domain network (DC-DuNet), a 2x2 configuration ablation controls branch width, depth and training schedule while varying these placements and their associated input channels. After 20 epochs, the PSNR contrasts are +1.98 dB for sinogram initialisation and -2.16 dB for the interpolated image base; the image-base ranking also reverses during training. The sinogram-initialised variant exceeds an image-only U-Net by 0.96 dB at 60 views and 10% dose, and by 1.28 dB on independently generated synthetic data. The U-Net leads at higher view counts and with a shared interpolated image base. Without retraining, the variant retains a 0.12 dB advantage on held-out CHAOS abdominal CT under simulated degradation; the image-base contrast rounds to zero. The implemented projection/backprojection pair has empirical dominant gain of approximately 0.85NK, where N is image-grid width and K acquired view count. These results distinguish interpolation placement from network architecture and support NK normalisation for step calibration.

</details>


### [185] [Decoder-as-a-Probe: Verbalizing and Steering Visual Representations in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34515)
*Adhemar de Senneville,Xavier Bou,Jérémy Anger,Rafael Grompone,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Decoder-as-a-Probe (DaP)，把 LVLM 的 LLM 解码器的一阶泰勒展开当作线性分类器来探测和操控视觉表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是解释大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）视觉表征中的可解释方向，即识别模型所见的概念（如颜色、服饰、动作）并据此描述或修改其感知。

Motivation: 经典线性探测依赖在表征上训练分类器，需要大量标注，且只能恢复可从表征中读出的信息，而非模型真正使用的信息。

Method: 核心洞见是 LVLM 的语言模型解码器本身即为以候选下一 token 为类别的分类器，故取其关于视觉表征的一阶泰勒展开作为线性分类器，无需额外训练即可得到探测方向，并用于语言化模型感知及定向修改特定属性。

实验效果: 摘要仅给出定性结论：DaP 尤其在训练数据稀疏时显著优于经典 probing 与 steering 基线，可语言化感知并精准修改颜色、服饰、动作等属性；未报告具体基准或数值。

Abstract: It is commonly accepted that high-level concepts are represented linearly in the representation space of large neural networks. For Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs), finding those interpretable directions in visual representations is important for interpretability. To find them, the classical approach is to train a linear classifier on the representations, but it needs a large amount of labels and only recovers what can be read from the representations, not what the model actually uses. In this paper we start from the following insight: The LLM Decoder of an LVLM is also a classifier whose classes are the possible next tokens. So we use its first-order Taylor expansion as a linear classifier. Hence, we introduce Decoder-as-a-Probe (DaP), which significantly outperforms classical probing and steering baselines especially when training data is sparse. (1) It verbalizes what an LVLM perceives in a given image. (2) It can surgically change the perception of the LVLM about specific attributes (e.g. change only the color, the clothes worn or the action done). Due to its simplicity and data efficiency, we believe DaP can serve as a plug-and-play tool for LVLM research, such as interpretability, hallucination mitigation or instruction tuning.

</details>


### [186] [PURA: Video Token Pruning via Unified Spatiotemporal Redundancy Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34521)
*Yuyao Sun,Hongli Yuan,Shuang Li,Tao Deng,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PURA 是一种无需训练的视觉 token 剪枝方法，通过统一的时空冗余评估与协调式批量剪枝，降低视频大模型推理成本并保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 视频大语言模型因长视觉 token 序列导致推理开销高；该任务以视频视觉 token 为输入，目标是压缩 token 数量，在视频理解性能与推理效率之间取得更优折中。

Motivation: 现有方法多利用帧内空间冗余与帧间时间冗余，但采用预设的处理顺序或固定的空时压缩配比，难以适应不同视频中空时冗余比例的动态变化。

Method: PURA 为每个 token 构建空间与时间参考候选，选取两维度中表示误差最低的参考；将该表示误差与 token 重要性结合，得到每个 token 的移除代价。为避免逐个移除最低代价 token 的时间开销，提出 Coordinated Pruning：迭代地将相互兼容的低代价移除分组为批次并行执行，每批后更新剪枝状态，直至达到目标预算。

实验效果: 在三个视频大语言模型和五个视频理解基准上的大量实验表明，PURA 在任务性能与推理效率之间取得了良好的折中；摘要未给出具体数值指标。

Abstract: Video large language models incur substantial inference costs from long visual token sequences. Existing video token compression methods mitigate these costs by exploiting spatial redundancy within frames and temporal redundancy across frames. However, many approaches impose predefined processing orders or fixed allocation ratios for spatial and temporal compression, limiting their ability to adapt to the varying balance of spatiotemporal redundancy across videos. To address this limitation, we propose PURA, a training-free method that prunes visual tokens through unified spatiotemporal redundancy assessment. For each token, PURA constructs spatial and temporal reference candidates and selects the reference with the lowest representation error across the two dimensions. The representation error is combined with token importance to determine each token's removal cost. To avoid the time overhead of removing the lowest-cost token one by one, we introduce Coordinated Pruning, which iteratively groups mutually compatible low-cost removals into batches for simultaneous execution and updates the pruning state after each batch until the target budget is reached. Extensive experiments on three video large language models and five video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that PURA achieves a favorable trade-off between task performance and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [187] [Rank Before You Encode: Reading a Few Percent of a Whole-Slide Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34523)
*Haoyu He,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在冻结的全切片图像（WSI）模型上用轻量选择器挑选少量切片编码，可在显著降低推理成本的同时基本保持AUROC，且优于九种高效方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 全切片图像分类需用病理基础模型编码数千个高分辨率切片（tile），编码占推理成本的主体；目标是在给定编码预算下以更低成本获得接近全袋（full-bag）推理的分类性能。

Motivation: 现有高效方法通过减少切片数量降本，但通常需围绕其选择策略重新训练模型；而"冻结已有全袋模型、仅在选定切片上以相同编码器成本运行"这一方案尚未被系统探索，也缺乏与等成本随机采样的对照基线。

Method: 用仅3.3K参数的轻量选择器拟合冻结模型的注意力，无需额外切片级标签；从切片缩略图排序区域，在5x下编码前K个区域，再在20x下选出b个送入冻结模型。作者将7个冻结MIL聚合器在6个任务上评估，并与同特征、同划分下训练的9种高效方法及等成本随机采样比较。

实验效果: 预算(K,b)=(1000,10)时编码成本约为全袋的6.6%、推理快10–14倍（不含切图），36个聚合器-任务对中33个AUROC下降不超过0.005；(500,10)时成本约4%、快13–22倍，但CAMELYON17上AUROC最多下降0.035（小转移灶易漏检）。选择在局灶性病变任务上比随机采样高约0.10 AUROC，而随机采样在弥漫性任务上表现良好；60组配对比较中(500,10)冻结模型在55组上优于9种高效方法。

Abstract: Classifying a whole-slide image requires encoding thousands of high-resolution tiles with a pathology foundation model, which accounts for most of the inference cost. Existing efficient methods reduce this cost by selecting fewer tiles, but typically train a new model around their selection strategy. A largely unexplored alternative is to keep an existing full-bag model frozen and run it on selected tiles at the same encoder cost. We introduce this comparison, alongside random tile sampling at matched cost. To select tiles, we fit a lightweight selector with 3.3K parameters to the attention of a frozen model, without additional slide labels. The selector ranks regions from a slide thumbnail, encodes the top K regions at 5x magnification, and selects b for processing at 20x. We evaluate seven frozen MIL aggregators on six tasks and compare them with nine efficient methods trained on the same features and splits. At a budget of (K,b)=(1000,10), encoding costs about 6.6% of the full bag and is 10-14x faster, excluding tiling. The AUROC drop is no more than 0.005 in 33 of 36 aggregator-task pairs. At (500,10), the cost falls to about 4% and inference is 13-22x faster, but AUROC drops by up to 0.035 on CAMELYON17, where small metastases are easily missed. Selection improves over random sampling by about 0.10 AUROC on tasks with focal lesions, while random sampling performs well on diffuse tasks. In our comparisons, the frozen model at (500,10) is ahead of nine efficient methods in 55 of 60 paired comparisons. Giving an existing WSI model fewer tiles is thus a baseline that a new efficient model should be compared with at matched encoder cost.

</details>


### [188] [HAS: Hierarchical Adaptive Frame Sampling for Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34544)
*Yuyao Sun,Daiqi Shi,Shuang Li,Liao Zhang,Yang Cai,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAS 是一个两阶段的分层自适应帧采样框架，先构建查询相关且非冗余的骨干帧，再依据查询需求自适应分配局部与全局上下文预算，以适配不同查询的证据需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 由于上下文长度限制，多模态大模型（MLLM）无法处理完整长视频，只能采样部分帧作为输入。该任务的目标是为给定查询与长视频选择有限的帧子集，使模型在固定帧预算下尽可能满足查询所需证据。

Motivation: 现有采样方法（从均匀采样到相关性选择）多由单一采样原则驱动，难以应对不同查询的异质证据需求：有的需要纵观视频整体演化，有的则依赖短时间窗口内的细粒度事件。

Method: 框架分两阶段。第一阶段"骨干帧构建"使用行列式点过程（DPP）选取既与查询相关又彼此非冗余的帧，覆盖视频关键片段并作为采样集合的基础。第二阶段"自适应上下文增强"分析骨干帧的时间分布以推断查询需求，将剩余帧预算在局部上下文与全局上下文之间自适应分配：局部上下文补充细粒度时序动态与短程因果关系，全局上下文连接时间上孤立的证据并提供整体视图以支持全局理解。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体数值结果；仅定性地说明将 HAS 集成到三个主流 MLLM 后，在 Video-MME、LongVideoBench 和 MLVU 三个基准上均取得一致更优的性能。

Abstract: Due to context-length constraints, most MLLMs cannot process full-length videos and therefore rely on sampling a subset of frames as input. However, existing sampling methods, ranging from uniform sampling to relevance-based selection, are often driven by a single sampling principle and thus struggle to accommodate the heterogeneous evidential requirements of different queries: some demand a holistic understanding of how the video unfolds over time, while others hinge on fine-grained events within a short temporal window. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{Hierarchical Adaptive Frame Sampling (HAS)}, a two-stage frame sampling framework. In the first stage, \emph{Backbone Frame Construction}, we apply a Determinantal Point Process (DPP) to sample frames that are both query-relevant and non-redundant. These selected frames capture key moments across the video and form the backbone of the sampling set, providing a foundation for subsequent enrichment. In the second stage, \emph{Adaptive Contextual Enrichment}, we analyze the temporal distribution of the backbone frames to infer the query's requirements and adaptively allocate the remaining frame budget between Local and Global Context. The Local Context enriches the backbone with fine-grained temporal dynamics and short-range causal relations, whereas the Global Context connects temporally isolated evidence and provides a holistic view of the entire video to support global understanding. Through such a hierarchical two-stage process, HAS effectively addresses diverse query requirements. Incorporated into three leading MLLMs, it demonstrates consistently superior performance across the Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU benchmarks.

</details>


### [189] [Recalling the Past to Answer the Present: Context-Aware Multi-Evidence Reasoning for Streaming Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34545)
*Cui Yakun,Tiantian Geng,Huixiang Kong,Xingqun Qi,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向流式视频问答的基准 StreamRecall、证据利用级联诊断框架，以及免训练因果视觉记忆机制 EviStream，用于评估与支撑跨时序证据的在线推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 流式视频问答要求模型在视频历史中检索并推理分散的上下文相关证据，在恰当时刻回答查询，输入为连续视频流与查询，输出为答案（及证据定位）。

Motivation: 现有 VQA 基准主要在离线设定下评测，无法反映真实流式场景中查询时间与上下文对齐、证据跨时间分散的需求，且缺乏对证据利用过程的细粒度诊断手段。

Method: 构建 StreamRecall 基准（462 个视频、4,983 个问题，含上下文对齐的查询时刻与细粒度时序证据标注），提出 Evidence Utilization Cascade 将推理拆解为证据定位、证据落地、证据链补全与答案推导四阶段以诊断模型；并提出免训练的因果视觉记忆机制 EviStream，在固定 KV 预算下保留推理关键证据以实现高效长时流式推理。

实验效果: 摘要报告实验显示当前 VLM 与真实流式需求之间存在显著差距，但未给出具体基准数值或对比结果。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in video understanding, yet existing video question answering (VQA) benchmarks mainly evaluate models in offline settings. Real-world streaming scenarios instead require models to answer context-dependent queries by retrieving and reasoning over evidence distributed across the video history. We introduce StreamRecall, a benchmark for context-aware Multi evidence reasoning in streaming video QA. StreamRecall requires models to retrieve, associate, and reason over temporally scattered evidence to answer queries at the appropriate moment. It contains 4,983 questions over 462 videos, with context-aligned query times and fine-grained temporal evidence annotations. We further propose the Evidence Utilization Cascade to diagnose model reasoning through evidence localization, grounding, chain completion, and answer derivation. To enable efficient long-term streaming reasoning, we present EviStream, a training-free causal visual memory mechanism that preserves reasoning-critical evidence under a fixed KV budget. Experiments reveal substantial gaps between current VLMs and realistic streaming requirements, establishing StreamRecall as a foundation for developing evidence-aware streaming video intelligence.

</details>


### [190] [CADENCE: Sensor-GroundedWorld-Consistent Gaussian SLAM for Collaborative Mobile Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34549)
*Reshad Ul Karim,Abrar Samin,Md Rashad Al Hasan Rony*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CADENCE 是一个协作式高斯 SLAM 系统，通过将跨设备匹配检索与观测是否及如何更新地图的决策解耦，提升异构消费级手机协同室内建图的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务利用多台消费级手机进行协作式高斯 SLAM，从各设备观测出发构建共享高斯地图并估计位姿，目标是更快、更完整地重建室内地图。

Motivation: 异构设备带来退化观测与不可靠的跨设备关联，单个劣质贡献者或错误匹配即可污染共享高斯地图，因此需要将"寻找匹配"与"是否及如何融合"分离处理。

Method: 匹配阶段用传感器锚定的区域记忆汇总已提交观测，并依据外观、相对位姿、重力、气压高程与观测质量对候选同伴区域排序，再由确定性几何验证与可选物理检查裁定接受与否；匹配接受后，按不同线索分别计算的置信度独立控制其对位姿约束、几何、外观与记忆的贡献，使颜色不可靠时深度仍可生效；同时本地跟踪与建图、验证、网络模块相互隔离。

实验效果: 在八栋未参与记忆训练的 Matterport3D 房屋上，区域记忆 AUROC 达 0.95–0.97 并优于外观检索，误连接率低于 0.5%；软置信度加权将八种退化下的平均 PSNR 损失从 2.66 dB 降至 1.05 dB；在十二段留出 iPhone 扫描上平均绝对轨迹误差为 6.98 cm，精化地图的平均渲染质量优于基线。

Abstract: Collaborative reconstruction with consumer phones promises faster and more complete indoor maps. However, heterogeneous devices introduce degraded observations and unreliable cross-device associations, so a single poor contributor or incorrect match can corrupt the shared Gaussian map. To address this, we propose \textbf{CADENCE}, a collaborative Gaussian simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) system that separates finding cross-device matches from deciding whether and how peer observations update the map. To find matches, a sensor-grounded regional memory summarizes committed observations and ranks candidate peer regions using appearance, relative pose, gravity, barometric altitude, and observation quality. Deterministic geometric verification and optional physical checks then decide which of the proposed matches are accepted. Once a match is accepted, separate cue-based confidences control how its observations contribute to pose constraints, geometry, appearance, and memory. As a result, useful depth can still contribute even when color is unreliable. Throughout this process, local tracking remains isolated from mapping, verification, and networking. We evaluate CADENCE on eight Matterport3D houses that were not used to train the memory. On these houses, regional memory achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of $0.95$ to $0.97$ and thus outperforms appearance retrieval, while verification keeps false-link rates below $0.5\,\%$. Furthermore, soft confidence weighting reduces the mean receiver peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) loss from $2.66$ to $1.05$\,dB across eight degradations while retaining useful peer depth. Finally, using inertial and platform motion, CADENCE tracks all twelve held-out iPhone scans with a mean absolute trajectory error of $6.98$\,cm, and its refined maps achieve better average rendering quality than the baselines.

</details>


### [191] [Score-based Black-box Attack via Adversarial Condition Guided Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34564)
*Yuhang Chen,Chih-Hong Cheng,Kurt Debattista,Siddartha Khastgir,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将基于分数的黑盒攻击适配到反向扩散过程，并用自适应目标/目标搜索引导提升生成式黑盒对抗攻击的效果与图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是在生成式模型框架下构造无限制对抗攻击：在保持原图自然外观与语义的前提下使分类器误判，输入为原始图像及受害分类器，输出为高质量的对抗样本，目标是兼顾攻击效果与图像质量。

Motivation: 现有基于扩散的黑盒攻击主要依赖替代模型的迁移性，效果对替代模型与受害模型间的差异敏感；提升迁移性又会扭曲语义内容、降低图像质量；同时沿用原提示词使扩散模型的条件先验未被用于对抗引导。

Method: 将基于分数的黑盒攻击适配到反向扩散中，仅用受害模型的输出概率引导潜变量更新而无需其梯度；引入自适应目标引导，以受害模型当前次优类作为对抗条件，将去噪导向决策边界；为抑制由此带来的语义偏移，进一步提出目标搜索引导，用基于分数的攻击搜索原条件嵌入的微小扰动，从而在保持攻击效果的同时改善图像质量。

实验效果: 在ImageNet-Compatible数据集上，该框架在攻击效果与图像质量两方面均优于基于迁移的基线方法；摘要未报告具体数值。此外，目标搜索引导在攻击效果与次优类引导相当的同时获得更好的图像质量。

Abstract: Generative models enable unrestricted adversarial attacks that aim to fool classifiers while preserving the natural appearance and semantics of the original image. However, existing diffusion-based black-box attacks largely rely on transferability from surrogates, making their effectiveness sensitive to differences between surrogate and victim. Improving transferability can also distort semantic content and degrade image quality. Moreover, retaining the original prompt to preserve fidelity leaves the diffusion model's conditional prior underused for adversarial guidance. We address these limitations by adapting score-based black-box attacks to reverse diffusion, using the victim's output probabilities to guide latent updates without accessing its gradients. We further introduce adaptive-target guidance, which uses the victim's current runner-up class as an adversarial condition to steer denoising towards the decision boundary. Because such guidance can introduce unwanted semantic changes, we also propose target-search guidance, which searches for a small perturbation of the original condition embedding using a score-based attack. This achieves comparable attack effectiveness with better image quality than runner-up guidance. Experiments on the ImageNet-Compatible dataset show that our framework outperforms transfer-based baselines in both attack effectiveness and image quality.

</details>


### [192] [Physics-Guided Generative Modelling of Oxy-Combustion Flame Images: From Conditional LSGANs to Denoising Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34568)
*Imane Er-rabhi,Gorkem Oztarlik,Jean Caudal,Renaud Gaudron*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文构建并对比了条件GAN与物理引导DDPM两类深度生成代理模型，用于将15维燃烧工况参数直接映射为热释放率火焰图像，以替代昂贵的OH*化学发光实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是建立从15维连续运行参数（热功率、旋流数、动量比、混合分数等）到512×64热释放率火焰图像的映射，基于OXYTEC双旋流非预混富氧燃烧数据库的1,609个实验样本训练，目标是实现快速的工业富氧燃烧烧嘴设计验证。

Motivation: 传统烧嘴设计验证需逐工况在仪表化台架上复现实验并成像，导致迭代式设计探索异常缓慢；现有方法缺乏可直接从工况参数生成火焰场的快速代理手段。

Method: 采用两种互补生成范式：自定义空间锚定条件最小二乘GAN（cLSGAN-L1-CBN），单次前向生成实现近实时推理；以及自定义物理引导去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM），迭代采样更契合流体热释放的平滑连续梯度。二者均在分层留出测试集上通过消融实验验证，并结合最近邻分析定位误差来源、开展参数敏感性研究。

实验效果: 优化后的cLSGAN达到RMSE 0.022、FID 20.68，单图生成约20 ms，但延长训练不改善纹理真实感；DDPM在40,000轮达到RMSE 0.019、FID 20.29，但单图约需30秒，比对抗代理慢三个数量级。最近邻分析表明最差误差集中于参数空间稀疏采样区域，敏感性研究显示对扰动输入响应平滑且物理一致。

Abstract: Validating industrial oxy-combustion burner designs traditionally requires costly experimental campaigns in which each operating condition is physically reproduced on an instrumented rig and imaged via OH* chemiluminescence, rendering iterative design exploration prohibitively slow. This work develops deep generative surrogate models that map a 15-dimensional vector of continuous operating parameters (thermal power, swirl numbers, momentum ratios, mixture fractions, etc.) directly to a $512\times64$ heat-release flame image, trained on 1,609 experimental samples from the OXYTEC dual-swirl non-premixed oxy-combustion database. Two complementary generative paradigms are investigated, as they offer opposite strengths for surrogate modelling: a custom spatially-anchored conditional least-squares GAN (cLSGAN-L1-CBN), whose single-pass generation enables near-instantaneous inference, and a custom physics-guided denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM), whose iterative sampling favours the smooth, continuous heat-release gradients of fluid flows. Both are validated through ablation on a stratified held-out test set. The optimized cLSGAN reaches RMSE 0.022 and FID 20.68 and generates an image in about 20 ms on a mobile workstation GPU, though extended training leaves its texture realism unchanged; the DDPM improves steadily over long training horizons, reaching RMSE 0.019 and FID 20.29 at 40,000 epochs, at the cost of roughly thirty seconds per image on the same hardware --- three orders of magnitude slower than the adversarial surrogate. A nearest-neighbour analysis traces the persistent worst-case errors to sparsely sampled regions of the parameter space, and a parameter-sensitivity study confirms smooth, physically consistent responses to perturbed inputs. These results demonstrate that carefully guided generative models can act as reliable visual surrogates for combustion experiments.

</details>


### [193] [Sage-Med: Scaling Medical Multimodal Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34575)
*Sage-Med Team,:,Ming Hu,Cheng Tang,Wei Li,Rongbin Li,Feilong Tang,Junru Hu,Hongze Zhu,Qiujuan Yan,Huanze Tang,Shaocong Wei,Siyuan Yan,Min Cen,Zhongxing Xu,Zhonghua Wang,Lie Ju,Zhen Yu,Wenjie Li,Shiyan Su,Jing Hao,Jingyong Liang,Diping Song,Fang Yan,Chenglong Ma,Junzhi Ning,Haochen Xue,Zanting Ye,Chenrun Wang,Jinjie Wei,Yi Xin,Jianghan Shen,Siqi Luo,Jiyao Liu,Huidong Xie,Jun Zhao,Rulin Zhou,Longquan Jiang,Ziyan Huang,Ying Chen,Hanxiao Zhang,Junyang Wu,Mouxiao Bian,Jiashi Lin,Chenyu Wang,Qi Qin,Jie Huang,Yiling Zhou,Jiawei Chen,Kefan Wang,Jingjing Hua,Yaoyu Liu,Huihui Xu,Tianbin Li,Jin Ye,Ziyang Chen,Shujian Gao,Zilin Lu,Jiangtao Yan,Changkai Ji,Wenhao Tang,Xinyi Zeng,Yuxing Chen,Hongwei Yang,Rongzhao Zhang,Jinru Ding,Yingcheng Wu,Zhenfei Yin,Jinzhe Li,Lu Lu,Yun Zhong,Zhishan Lin,Bingli Wang,Xiaohan Ren,Jianmin Qian,Penghao Zhao,Zhongbo Tian,Zhenbang Zhou,Peiling Dai,Mengjing Zhao,Jingyu Zheng,Pinan Liu,Shiwei Li,Victoria Mar,Cristina Vico-Alonso,Qiang Wu,Xianming Liu,Lingtong Huang,Dili Song,Minghui Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Qipeng Guo,Yuqiang Li,Nanqing Dong,Yun Gu,Qi Duan,Hongliang Ren,Hongming Shan,Yin Xie,Xiang An,Benyou Wang,Yueming Su,Yefeng Zheng,Zongyuan Ge,Chun-Mei Feng,Jie Xu,Yu Qiao,Junjun He,Yirong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出35B参数（3B激活）的医疗多模态模型Sage-Med-Preview，通过分阶段数据筛选与渐进式训练课程实现可扩展的临床多模态智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是建立医疗多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的有效规模定律。输入为322.7亿条候选记录经阶段化筛选后的多模态医疗数据，输出为具备视觉理解与医学语言能力的医疗多模态模型，目标是在广泛临床任务上接近或超越前沿通用与专有模型。

Motivation: 系统性评估显示现有医疗MLLM在多种医疗任务上普遍不如前沿通用MLLM；单纯扩大模型规模主要提升文本能力而非多模态感知，且开启推理模式也无法弥合视觉能力差距，因此需要实证探究医疗多模态智能的实际扩展规律。

Method: 构建35B总参数、3B激活参数的Sage-Med-Preview：对3.227亿条候选记录做阶段特定的数据筛选，并采用渐进式训练课程，依次为医疗预训练、分阶段监督微调与自适应强化学习；实验表明渐进式监督精修与自适应强化学习在各阶段带来一致增益，同时提升专门视觉理解与医学语言能力。

实验效果: 在61个公开与13个私立多中心基准、40余种模态的238项临床任务上对比77个模型：31个公开多模态基准平均得分58.26%，高于开源医疗模型Hulu-Med-235B-A22B（50.99%）与通用科学模型Intern-S2-Preview（52.24%），并在综合多模态理解、报告生成、视觉定位等任务上接近专有模型GPT-6.2-Sol；在复杂医学推理与多步临床流程上仍存在明显挑战。

Abstract: Establishing effective scaling laws for medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remains a persistent and fundamental challenge. Our systematic evaluation shows that existing medical MLLMs tend to underperform frontier general-purpose MLLMs across a range of medical tasks, while scaling model size primarily improves text capabilities rather than multimodal perception, and enabling reasoning modes fails to close this visual gap. To investigate how medical multimodal intelligence scales in practice, we introduce Sage-Med-Preview, a 35B-parameter medical multimodal model with 3B activated parameters, built by combining stage-specific curation over 322.7 million candidate records with a progressive training curriculum spanning medical pretraining, staged supervised fine-tuning, and adaptive reinforcement learning. Across this curriculum, progressive supervised refinement and adaptive reinforcement learning yield consistent stage-wise gains, substantially advancing both specialized visual understanding and medical language capabilities. Evaluated against 77 competing models across 238 clinical tasks spanning 61 public and 13 private multi-center benchmarks in more than 40 modalities, Sage-Med-Preview achieves an average score of 58.26% on the 31 public multimodal benchmarks. It outperforms the state-of-the-art open-source medical model Hulu-Med-235B-A22B at 50.99% and the general scientific model Intern-S2-Preview at 52.24%, and approaches the advanced proprietary model GPT-6.2-Sol across multiple tasks such as comprehensive multimodal understanding, report generation, and visual grounding. Although substantial challenges remain in complex medical reasoning and multi-step clinical workflows, this report provides empirical evidence, actionable data and training recipes, and a rigorous benchmark toward scalable clinical multimodal intelligence.

</details>


### [194] [ADClaw: A Programmable Visual Agent for Industrial Anomaly Detection and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34591)
*Xinyi Wu,Rongxin Gao,YuHui Lin,Yonglin Hong,Chu Li,Wei Li,Dingzhao Li,Shijia Zhao,Chaopeng Li,Haixin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ADClaw 是一个无需任务特定微调、通过可执行代码反复检验异常假设的工业异常检测智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 工业异常检测（IAD）旨在识别并定位偏离正常产品状态的区域，是制造自动化质量检测的核心任务；目标是在2D/3D图像上输出异常判定与定位结果。

Motivation: 现有面向IAD的视觉语言模型依赖大规模领域数据集微调，且仅做单次前向预测，无法复查可疑区域；虽有可复用中间结果工具智能体，但预定义的工具接口限制了后续检查中对中间结果的灵活组合，导致细节缺陷漏检或缺陷类型误判。

Method: 提出ADClaw：对每个异常假设，先由结构化正常性先验（Structured Normality Prior）确定适用规则并检索正常视觉参考；在此先验引导下，智能体在持久化“检测工作台”（Inspection Workbench）中编写并执行代码，将先前步骤的掩码、异常图与测量量组合为新的检验；每次检验后由假设引导证据推理（Hypothesis-Guided Evidence Reasoning）判断证据是否支持该缺陷及其位置，并利用证据缺口或不一致引导下一步代码操作。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体数值；作者称ADClaw在2D与3D工业异常检测基准的检测与定位上取得有竞争力的结果，并在MMAD的异常理解任务上表现良好。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection (IAD), the task of identifying and localizing deviations from normal product conditions, is essential to automated quality inspection in manufacturing. Many vision-language models for IAD rely on large domain-specific datasets for fine-tuning and make predictions in a single pass, leaving no opportunity to re-examine ambiguous regions. Although tool-using agents can revisit these regions, predefined tool interfaces limit how they can combine intermediate results in a follow-up check. When such checks are insufficient, models may miss subtle defects or misidentify the defect type. To address this limitation, we introduce ADClaw, an inspection agent that uses executable code to test anomaly hypotheses without task-specific fine-tuning. For each hypothesis, a Structured Normality Prior identifies the applicable rules and retrieves normal visual references for the inspection target. Guided by this prior, the agent writes and executes code in a persistent Inspection Workbench to combine masks, anomaly maps, and measurements from earlier steps into a new test of the suspected defect. After each check, Hypothesis-Guided Evidence Reasoning assesses whether the evidence supports the suspected defect and its location, using gaps or inconsistencies in that evidence to guide the next code action. ADClaw achieves competitive results in detection and localization across 2D and 3D industrial anomaly detection benchmarks and in anomaly understanding on MMAD.

</details>


### [195] [SEAM: A Self-Evolving and Adaptive Probing Framework for Automatic MLLM Hallucination Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34598)
*Shiyu Zhang,Zengyi Wo,Yuming Liao,Ran He,Caifeng Shan,Yanbo Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEAM 提出一种自演化、自适应探测框架，自动生成并迭代优化测试项以评估多模态大模型的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该论文面向多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）幻觉的自动评估任务：以模型为输入，自动构建幻觉探测测试项并输出评估结果，目标是建立可靠、可持续更新的幻觉评测范式。

Motivation: 现有评测流程依赖预定义测试项，构建成本高、随模型迭代迅速过时，且难以针对特定模型的薄弱环节进行有效探测。

Method: SEAM 构建可组合的幻觉探测构建库（10 种幻觉探测技能、3 种诱导封装、5 种问题格式），并设计双环框架：内环进行自适应探测，协调技能引导的视觉分析、问题组合与分层质量验证，并借助验证器与目标模型响应反馈精炼测试项；外环进行技能演化，由技能更新器将历史探测轨迹蒸馏为局部可复用规则更新，经留出验证后在测试前冻结。

实验效果: 在 4 个目标 MLLM 与 10 类幻觉类型上的实验显示，SEAM 显著优于代表性静态基准，在 12 组匹配对比中平均有效幻觉发现率取得 89.2% 的相对提升。

Abstract: The severe susceptibility of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to hallucinations poses a critical threat to their reliability, making a rigorous evaluation framework indispensable. However, existing evaluation pipelines largely rely on predefined test items, suffering from high construction costs, rapid obsolescence as MLLMs evolve, and ineffectiveness in probing model-specific weaknesses. To address these limitations, we propose SEAM, a self-evolving and adaptive probing framework for automatic MLLM hallucination evaluation. Specifically, SEAM introduces a composable Hallucination Probe Construction Library comprising ten hallucination-probing skills, three elicitation wrappers, and five question formats. Building on this library, SEAM establishes a dual-loop framework. In the inner loop (adaptive probing), skill-guided visual analysis, question composition, and hierarchical quality verification are coordinated with verifier and target-response feedback to refine test items and uncover model-specific hallucinations. In the outer loop (skill evolution), a skill updater distills historical probing trajectories into localized, reusable rule updates, which undergo held-out validation and are frozen before testing. Driven by these innovative structural designs, SEAM establishes an automated and self-evolving evaluation paradigm. Experiments across four target MLLMs and ten hallucination types demonstrate that SEAM substantially outperforms representative static benchmarks, achieving an 89.2\% relative improvement in mean valid hallucination discovery rate across 12 matched comparisons.

</details>


### [196] [You Only Look Multiple Times: YoloMT, a Real-Time Streaming Model on a T4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34601)
*Nessim Ben Abbes,Duc Han Le,Sabri Mtibaa,Van-Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YoloMT 是一个 449.8M 参数的单 GPU 实时流式视频字幕模型，通过每帧仅编码一次并缓存为 16 个 token 来实现高效多帧利用，在多个流式视频基准上超越同规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是在单 GPU 上进行实时流式视频字幕生成（streaming captioning）：模型需持续接收视频帧流并输出描述性字幕，目标是低延迟、低算力下兼顾效果。

Motivation: 现有生成式视觉模型在处理流式长视频时，重复对历史帧做视觉编码代价高昂，难以在 1B 以下规模同时兼顾效率与性能；作者希望在该规模区间取得帕累托前沿表现。

Method: 从 27B 教师模型经 SFT 与 GRPO 蒸馏得到 449.8M 学生模型；关键设计是每帧只编码一次并缓存为 16 个 token，使模型可复用并推理历史帧而无需额外视觉前向，从而以低成本读入更多帧且保持信息量；论文还给出分阶段训练流程、数据配方及各组件的消融实验。

实验效果: 在 OVO-Bench、MVBench、StreamingBench 和 Video-MME（基于其生成字幕作答）上，YoloMT 在 1B 参数以下模型中均领先，并优于 LFM2.5-VL-1.6B 与 SmolVLM2-2.2B。

Abstract: We introduce a new model for real-time streaming captioning on a single GPU. YoloMT is a 449.8M-parameter streaming captioner that provides Pareto-frontier performance among generative vision models in its class. Distilled from a 27B teacher by SFT and GRPO, it leads every model under 1B parameters on OVO-Bench, MVBench, StreamingBench and Video-MME, answered from its captions, and beats LFM2.5-VL-1.6B and SmolVLM2-2.2B. YoloMT is designed to read more frames cheaply and make them informative, encoding each frame once and caching it as 16 tokens. This allows the model to incorporate and infer from past frames without additional vision passes, making it efficient and effective. We also provide a detailed recipe for training YoloMT, with its stages and data, as well as ablations that demonstrate the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [197] [VisualMontage: Manipulating the Beliefs of Vision-Language Agents with Authentic Image Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34603)
*Jinwei Hu,Lai Wei,Zhuoyun Li,Youcheng Sun,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"视觉认知合谋"这一针对视觉语言模型智能体的新型认知威胁，构建基准与VisualMontage多智能体攻击模拟框架，并在12个模型上验证其跨模型可迁移性与防御残留风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 随着基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的智能体越来越多地解读社交媒体内容，本文研究"视觉认知合谋"问题：由协同发布的帖子将真实图片与局部忠实的配文组合，诱导VLM智能体通过自身推理接受攻击者预设的目标主张。为此作者构建了包含544个目标案例、覆盖8个应用领域、33.2K张候选图像的新基准，用于系统评估该威胁。

Motivation: 现有工作缺乏对"真实素材+局部忠实配文"协同诱导智能体推理结论这一认知层面威胁的系统刻画与评测手段，也缺少可复现的攻击模拟方法与跨模型、跨防御的量化证据，因而难以支撑相应防护机制的设计。

Method: 提出VisualMontage多智能体框架，通过叙事规划、图像选择与排序、配文分配并在迭代评审下生成攻击信息流，从而在新基准上模拟协同发帖攻击。实验在12个视觉语言模型上从模态效应、生产团队组件与下游防御三个维度展开评估，并对所有模型使用相同攻击信息流、不做针对受害模型的优化，以检验跨模型迁移性。

实验效果: 在无受害者定制优化条件下实现跨模型迁移，专有模型平均攻击成功率达61.93%、开源权重模型达68.71%，且92.65%的生成信息流通过独立模型化有效性审核；即使经下游审查，残留攻击成功率仍为35.3%–76.7%。受控对比还显示，配文可提升模型评定的发帖自然度，但同时降低攻击成功率。

Abstract: As agents based on vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly interpret social-media content, errors in their analysis may lend credibility to rumors and amplify their spread. In this paper, we study the problem of visual cognitive collusion, in which coordinated posts combine authentic images and locally faithful captions to induce VLM agents to infer and accept attacker-chosen target claims through their own reasoning. To enable systematic evaluation of this cognitive threat, we introduce a new benchmark comprising 544 target cases and 33.2K candidate images across eight application domains. We further propose VisualMontage, a multi-agent framework for simulating such attacks through narrative planning, image selection and ordering, and caption assignment under iterative review. We evaluate this threat through extensive experiments across 12 vision-language models, examining modality effects, production-team components, and downstream defenses. Using identical attack feeds across models without victim-specific optimization, we demonstrate cross-model transferability, achieving mean attack success rates of 61.93% for proprietary models and 68.71% for open-weight models. Moreover, 92.65% of generated feeds pass independent model-based validity auditing. Even after downstream review, residual attack success rates range from 35.3% to 76.7% across the evaluated configurations. Our additional controlled comparisons further reveal that captions can improve model-rated posting naturalness while reducing attack success. Together, these findings reveal a novel cognitive threat in social-media interpretation and motivate safeguards that assess how authentic posts jointly shape agents' reasoning and conclusions.

</details>


### [198] [DuCo: Exploiting Dual Cross-Layer Consistency for Sparse Geometric Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34608)
*Liangyu Xiang,Junyu Gao,Xiaofeng Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练稀疏注意力框架 DuCo，利用跨层注意力一致性与 value 冗余实现视觉几何基础模型的高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 视觉几何基础模型依赖稠密全局注意力捕获跨视图几何关系，但二次复杂度使长图像序列推理代价高昂；任务目标是在保持精度的前提下降低注意力计算开销，输入为长图像序列，输出为稀疏注意力掩码下的高效推理结果。

Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法仅依据注意力概率选取全局注意力矩阵元素，且各层独立构建掩码，忽视了两点：value 表示的冗余性，以及跨层的注意力信息复用潜力。

Method: 先分析注意力分布并对跨层 value 表示聚类，揭示两种跨层一致性（注意力概率分布高度相似、value 冗余结构局部变化）；据此提出免训练框架 DuCo，用历史注意力估计当前层稠密注意力概率，并用 value 聚类刻画冗余、通过跨层局部更新高效精炼簇；最终以概率加权 value 失真统一两类因素，在注意力预算约束下联合优化四种簇表示状态以构建稀疏掩码。

实验效果: 在三个视觉几何基础模型与五个基准上实验，展现出良好的精度—效率权衡；摘要未报告具体数值指标。

Abstract: Dense global attention is crucial for capturing cross-view geometry in visual geometry foundation models, yet its quadratic complexity limits efficient inference on long image sequences. Existing sparse attention methods primarily select entries of the global attention matrix based on attention probabilities and construct masks independently across layers, overlooking value redundancy and cross-layer information reuse. By analyzing attention distributions and clustering value representations across layers, we reveal two forms of \emph{cross-layer consistency}, characterized by highly similar attention probability distributions and localized changes in value redundancy structures. Building on these observations, we propose \textbf{DuCo}, a training-free sparse attention framework that exploits historical attention information and value redundancy for efficient geometric inference. Specifically, DuCo leverages historical attention information to estimate current-layer dense attention probabilities and introduces value clustering to exploit redundancy, with clusters efficiently refined through local updates across layers. Both factors are integrated through probability-weighted value distortion to jointly optimize four cluster representation states under an attention budget for sparse mask construction. Experiments across three visual geometry foundation models and five benchmarks demonstrate favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-offs.

</details>


### [199] [Commit2Drive: Perception-Grounded Language Decisions as Commitments for Trajectory Planning in Vision-Language-Action Driving Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34614)
*Zikai Zhang,Hubert P. Shum,Toby Breckon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Commit2Drive 将语言决策作为轨迹规划的一致性约束，把决策定义为可由轨迹确定规则读出的带连续参数的机动类别码，从而在闭环驾驶中保证语言与动作一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: VLA 模型把语言推理引入端到端自动驾驶，同时输出语言决策与轨迹以提升可解释性；输入为感知信息，输出为语言决策和规划轨迹，目标是让二者在闭环驾驶中保持一致且可执行。

Motivation: 现有方法中语言决策或为独立输出、或仅作为在相似候选轨迹间选择的索引，且其标注未与轨迹核验，导致闭环驾驶中语言决策与轨迹经常不一致。

Method: 将决策定义为“承诺”：机动类别加连续参数，由单一确定性规则从任意轨迹读出，并基于模型自身感知报告的事实接地；Maneuver Bank 把承诺映射为具运动学含义的代码，一致性损失训练规划器执行场景中每种可行决策；自动数据流水线用专家未来运动标注决策、以反事实执行联合训练决策与轨迹、并用规则函数配合 VLM 生成、审查和精炼语言标注；代码查找不依赖语言模型隐状态，故可扩展至规则决策而无需重训练。

实验效果: 在 Bench2Drive 上，使用 0.5B 语言模型与轻量模型取得 DS 78.50、SR 55.91%，与当前最优方法具有竞争力，并大幅降低语言-动作不一致；摘要未报告具体的不一致性数值对比。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models introduce language reasoning into end-to-end autonomous driving. They output a language decision together with a trajectory, which makes the planner more interpretable. However, in existing methods the language decision is either an independent output or an index that selects among similar candidate trajectories, and its annotations are not verified against the trajectory. As a result, the language decision and the trajectory are often inconsistent in closed-loop driving. To address this, we propose Commit2Drive, a framework that imposes the language decision as a consistency constraint on trajectory planning. The decision is a commitment, a maneuver class with a continuous parameter that one deterministic rule reads from any trajectory, and it is grounded in facts reported by the perception of the model itself. A Maneuver Bank maps the commitment to a code with a kinematic meaning, and consistency losses train the planner to execute every admissible decision of a scene, so that the trajectory follows the decision. An automatic data pipeline supplies the supervision. Decisions are labeled from the future motion of the expert, counterfactual executions train the decision and the trajectory together, and rule-based functions with a Vision-Language Model (VLM) generate, review and refine the language annotations. The code is a lookup that involves no hidden state of the language model, so Commit2Drive is extensible to rule-based decisions, which the same planner executes without retraining. Commit2Drive uses a 0.5B language model and runs closed-loop inference online on a single consumer graphics card. On Bench2Drive, Commit2Drive reaches a Driving Score (DS) of 78.50 and a Success Rate (SR) of 55.91% with a lightweight model, which is competitive with the state of the art, and greatly reduces the language-action inconsistency. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [200] [Beyond Pose Control: Anatomically Consistent Ultrasound Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34622)
*Yue Zhou,Fangxu Han,Tony Danjun Wang,Nassir Navab,Yuan Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出在相机可控视频生成框架下，为超声生成器注入"解剖先验"与"3D潜空间记忆"两类知识，以解决仅靠姿态控制导致的解剖不合理与重访不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是相机可控视频生成在超声领域的对应问题：以探头姿态作为视角，预测场景在新视角下的成像。实际场景中已追踪的超声扫描数据稀缺，目标是生成具有合理解剖结构、姿态跟随准确且重访一致的新姿态超声图像。

Motivation: 相机可控视频生成依赖大规模数据隐式学习先验，而超声领域恰好相反：解剖学提供了显式先验，但带追踪的扫描数据太少，无法隐式学习。因此仅靠姿态条件不足，现有姿态条件生成器在未扫描区域产生不合理解剖，在重访视角产生不一致解剖。

Method: 为生成器提供构成"超声医师心智模型"的两类知识：(1) 来自人群受试者构建的解剖先验，在未扫描区域引导生成，并由每个生成的片段在线微调以趋向扫描对象；(2) 围绕超声横截面物理特性设计的3D潜空间记忆，将生成切片合成为患者体积并在新姿态下重切片，保证重访解剖一致。细粒度记忆在输入端注入生成器，粗粒度解剖先验跨transformer块引导布局。

实验效果: 在甲状腺、肾脏和腰椎数据集上，相比姿态条件基线与记忆增强基线，在视觉保真度、姿态跟随和解剖一致性上均有提升；摘要未报告具体数值指标。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation anticipates how a scene appears from new viewpoints by relying on a prior learned implicitly from large-scale data. Ultrasound poses the same problem, with the probe pose as the viewpoint, but under opposite conditions: anatomy offers an explicit prior that every sonographer relies on, whereas tracked scans are too scarce to learn it implicitly. As a result, pose control alone is not enough, and pose-conditioned ultrasound generators produce implausible anatomy in unscanned regions and inconsistent anatomy on revisits. We go beyond pose control and give the generator the two kinds of knowledge that make up a sonographer's mental model. An anatomical prior, built from a population of subjects, guides generation where nothing has been scanned, and is refined online by every generated chunk towards the scanned subject. A 3D latent memory, designed around the cross-sectional physics of ultrasound, compounds the generated slices into a volume of the patient and reslices it at new poses, keeping revisited anatomy consistent. The fine-grained memory enters the generator at the input, while the coarse anatomy prior guides the layout across transformer blocks. Experiments on thyroid, kidney, and lumbar spine show improved visual fidelity, pose following, and anatomical consistency over pose-conditioned and memory-augmented baselines.

</details>


### [201] [Carrying Feature Ablations Across Sparse Autoencoder Seeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34637)
*Karl Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过移除SAE特征并比较跨随机种子的编码器输出变化，检验特征匹配是否具有功能等价性，发现仅部分匹配对可迁移，并指出层贡献比方向更利于预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 视觉编码器逐层将图像块编码为向量，稀疏自编码器（SAE）把某一层向量表示为学习到的方向（特征）之和。本文以DINOv2或SigLIP的某层及其SAE为设定，输入为不同随机种子训练的两个SAE的特征匹配对，输出为这些匹配对在特征移除后是否"跨种子携带"的判定。

Motivation: 已有工作仅依据方向一致性对两个SAE的特征做一对一匹配，但方向相近的特征可能响应图像的不同区域，因此移除匹配特征对编码器的影响可能不同，方向相似度不足以说明特征在功能上可互换。

Method: 先移除某特征，再单独移除其匹配特征，在相同留出图像上比较编码器输出的变化；若第二次变化几乎复现第一次，则判定该移除跨种子携带。设置容忍度，并在两个对照中检验：方向保持随机的SAE，以及事后将训练特征间匹配打乱。随后事后评估以层贡献与以方向预测"携带"的准确率。

实验效果: 在三种设定下，12%–70%的匹配对满足携带判据，而两个对照均为0%。事后预测中，使用层贡献比使用方向更准确，但仍存在错误预测，其中一个在独立图像上依然存在，说明可在层层面筛选匹配对，而测量输出能发现该筛选遗漏的错误。

Abstract: A vision encoder turns an image into one vector per patch, refined layer by layer. A sparse autoencoder (SAE) writes each vector of one layer as a sum of learned directions, most with a coefficient of zero. Researchers call each direction a feature and edit the encoder by removing one feature. An SAE trained from another random seed learns different features. Earlier work matches the two SAEs' features one to one by how closely their directions agree. Matched features can point alike yet respond to different image parts, so removing them may change the encoder differently. We remove a feature and, separately, its match, then compare the changes in the encoder's output on the same held out images. When the second change nearly reproduces the first, we say the removal carries across seeds. A setting fixes a DINOv2 or SigLIP layer and its SAE feature count. At our tolerance, the removal carries for 12% to 70% of matched pairs across three settings. The share is 0% in two controls: an SAE whose directions stay random and, post hoc, matches shuffled among trained features. Post hoc, predicting which removals carry is more accurate with layer contributions than with directions. Some incorrect predictions remain. One persists on independent images. So pairs can be screened at the layer. Measuring the output detects errors this screen misses.

</details>


### [202] [PRAM: Activating Latent Multimodal Knowledge through Partwise Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34640)
*Haidong Chen,Ying Guo,Shangze Li,Zhongkuan Mao,Linrui Xu,Canran Xiao,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模态部件化推理框架PRAM，借助共享枢轴模态组合部件级统计关系，在冻结编码器上激活潜在多模态知识，实现跨模态分类与检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该研究面向涌现式跨模态分类与检索任务，即在无直接成对监督的情况下实现模态间知识迁移；输入为多模态数据（经冻结编码器提取的特征），输出为类别预测与候选排序结果，目标是提升跨模态匹配性能。

Motivation: 现有方法侧重样本级对齐，忽视局部判别信息与部件间统计关系，导致跨模态迁移中细粒度判别结构未被充分利用，因而需要一种能够建模部件级统计关联的推理框架。

Method: PRAM包含三个模块：1）多粒度协方差正则化模块，在两组成对集合（模态-枢轴）上由全局与局部向量估计协方差，并通过与跨部件均值的自适应混合稳定局部估计；2）基于枢轴的谱关系推理模块，通过正则化协方差组合推断目标模态间的全局与部件级统计关系，并利用白化与谱分解提取成对投影方向及相关系数强度；3）多粒度分数融合模块，对全局与局部关系的匹配分数进行标准化与融合，并融入枢轴关联与类别语义的任务特定信息，用于类别预测与候选排序。

实验效果: 在十个数据集上的大量实验表明，PRAM在16种分类设置与19种检索设置中取得最优性能，并在三种感知场景中表现极具竞争力。

Abstract: Emergent cross-modal classification and retrieval enable transfer between modalities without direct paired supervision. However, existing methods often prioritize sample-level alignment, overlooking local discriminative information and partwise statistical relations. To this end, we propose partwise reasoning across modalities (PRAM), which activates latent multimodal knowledge in frozen encoders by composing partwise statistical relations through a shared pivot modality. Specifically, we first present a multi-granularity covariance regularization module that estimates covariances from global and local vectors in two collections of modality-pivot pairs, stabilizing local estimates through adaptive blending with their cross-part means. Second, we devise a pivot-based spectral relational reasoning module that infers global and partwise statistical relations between target modalities through regularized covariance composition and extracts paired projection directions and their correlation strengths via whitening and spectral decomposition. Finally, we introduce a multi-granularity score fusion module that standardizes and fuses matching scores from global and local relations, incorporating task-specific information on pivot associations and class semantics for class prediction and candidate ranking. Extensive experiments across ten datasets demonstrate that PRAM achieves state-of-the-art performance in 16 classification settings and 19 retrieval settings, while exhibiting highly competitive performance in three sensing scenarios.

</details>


### [203] [Motion Beyond Pixels: Learning Future Visual Representations for Object-Centric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34644)
*Chengzhi Zhao,Juelin Li,Zhennan Wang,Sheng Wang,Zhuoman Liu,Yuhao Lu,Jiongxu Chen,Haitang Feng,Yanjie Li,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionRep 是一个联合生成未来 DINO 表征与 3D 物体流的视觉表征世界模型，以语义—几何耦合的物体中心动力学预测未来状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务面向具身世界模型中的未来状态预测：以初始观测、物体查询点和语言指令为输入，输出未来视觉表征与 3D 物体流，目标是支撑物体中心的运动生成与场景泛化。

Motivation: 现有世界模型多在面向 RGB 重建优化的隐空间中预测未来，该目标未显式优先建模与运动生成相关的物体级语义与几何，导致表征与动力学建模需求错位。

Method: 提出 DINO-VAE，通过时间与通道压缩加隐变量正则化，将高维 DINO 特征压成紧凑且保留语义几何信息的隐表示；再用统一扩散 Transformer 通过联合注意力同时预测未来视觉表征与 3D 物体流；并采用非对称的模态特定噪声调度，使视觉流在联合去噪中领先于 3D 物体流，以逐步细化的视觉表征引导流生成。

实验效果: 在仿真与真实世界操作基准上，MotionRep 在 3D 物体流精度和对未见场景的泛化上优于基于像素的基线，且推理仅需三步去噪。

Abstract: Many embodied world models predict future states in latent spaces optimized for RGB reconstruction, which do not explicitly prioritize object-level semantics and geometry relevant to motion generation. We present MotionRep, a visual representation world model that jointly generates future DINO representations and 3D object flow, coupling semantic and geometric information with object-centric dynamics. To make high-dimensional DINO features amenable to generative modeling, we introduce DINO-VAE, which combines temporal and channel compression with latent regularization to produce compact latents while preserving semantic and geometric information. Conditioned on an initial observation, object query points, and a language instruction, a unified diffusion transformer predicts future visual representations and 3D object flow through joint attention. MotionRep employs asymmetric modality-specific noise schedules to keep the visual stream ahead of 3D object flow during joint denoising, allowing progressively refined future visual representations to guide flow generation. Experiments on simulated and real-world manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that MotionRep outperforms pixel-based baselines in 3D object flow accuracy and generalization to unseen scenes, while requiring only three denoising steps for inference. These results support predictive visual representations as an effective future-state space for object-centric dynamics modeling.

</details>


### [204] [A Hard Background Pairing Transfers across Classifiers and Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34645)
*Karl Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文检验了"最难配对"复合基准的可迁移性：由一半分类器选出的最难对象-背景配对，在未参与选择的另一半分类器和新照片上仍然显著更难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是对复合图像（从照片中抠出的物体粘贴到其他照片的背景上）评估图像分类器，输入为40个ImageNet分类器及COCO中的物体与背景标签，输出是可用于横向比较分类器的固定困难配对基准，目标是衡量分类器在非常规物体-背景组合下的失效。

Motivation: 现有复合基准中被证明的"最难配对"只是对某些分类器最难，且可能只对所挑选时用的那些照片难，因此其作为严格测试的有效性与可迁移性存疑——它可能无法区分其他模型，也不适用于新图像。

Method: 将40个ImageNet分类器按报告的ImageNet准确率均衡分成两半；一半从COCO的8个物体标签与8个背景标签中选出最难配对；另一半完全未参与选择，将该配对与同物体、同背景的随机配对进行比较，并在新照片生成的复合图上评估。

实验效果: 对14个在物体贴回原背景后净准确率变化很小的留出分类器，其在新照片复合图上的平均准确率从随机配对的50.3%降至最难配对的43.7%，低6.6个百分点，表明最难配对可跨模型、跨照片迁移并可用于固定基准比较。

Abstract: An image classifier names the object in an image, and its accuracy is the share of images named correctly. A classifier that names a car on a road may miss the same car pasted onto clouds. Composite benchmarks measure such failures by pasting objects cut from photographs onto backgrounds from other photographs. A pairing puts every object label on a different background label, such as truck on sea. The pairing on which some classifiers do worst, their hardest pairing, makes a strict test, but may be hard only for them or only on the photographs used to choose it. We show that the hardest pairing of some classifiers stays hard for others and on new photographs. We split 40 ImageNet classifiers into halves balanced by reported ImageNet accuracy. One half picks its hardest pairing of 8 object and 8 background labels from Common Objects in Context (COCO). The other half, held out of the choice, compares that pairing with random pairings of the same objects and backgrounds. For the 14 held-out classifiers with little net accuracy change after pasting objects back onto their own backgrounds, mean accuracy on composites from new photographs falls from 50.3% over random pairings to 43.7% on the hardest pairing, 6.6 percentage points lower. A benchmark using these composites can compare this pool's held-out classifiers on the fixed and random pairings.

</details>


### [205] [Atunda: Building an Afrogenic Dance Movement Dataset for AI-Driven Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34649)
*Diego Carrascosa,Tristan Pank,Qudus Onikeku,Mas'ud Onikeku,Jeremy A Frusco,Osubi Craig,Angelos Barmpoutis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发布首个系统性采集的 Afrogenic（非洲及非洲散居群体）舞蹈动作视频数据集 Atunda，并基于该数据集训练时序卷积网络实现超过95%的动作分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务为舞蹈动作视频分类：以 Afrogenic 表演性文化中的舞蹈视频为输入，输出对应动作类别标签，目标是构建可用于深度学习模型训练与评测的资源并为该领域建立性能基准。

Motivation: Afrogenic 表演性文化与传统在动作分类研究中长期缺乏关注，此前也不存在经过系统性采集、可直接用于 AI 训练与评测的该类舞蹈数据集，这一资源空白限制了相关建模与评测的开展。

Method: 作者构建 Atunda 数据集，第一阶段收录近400段视频、涵盖非洲与非洲散居表演性文化中13种不同舞蹈动作；随后在该数据上训练并评测多种配置的时序卷积神经网络，以此建立动作分类的基线。

实验效果: 时序卷积网络多种配置的分类准确率均超过95%，为 Afrogenic 表演性文化这一研究不足领域的后续动作分类研究提供了基准；摘要未报告与其他数据集的对比结果。

Abstract: This paper introduces Atunda, the first systematically collected video dataset of Afrogenic dance moves, designed for training and evaluating deep learning models for movement classification. The first phase of the dataset includes nearly 400 videos covering 13 distinct dance moves from African and African Diaspora performative culture, making it the first AI-ready resource of its kind. Using this dataset, we train and evaluate various configurations of temporal convolutional neural networks, achieving a classification accuracy exceeding 95%. The baseline results provide a benchmark for future studies in movement classification, especially in the understudied area of Afrogenic performative culture and tradition. This work lays the foundation for developing in the future digital metrics of similarity, automated attribution systems, and tools that preserve and promote the cultural heritage of Afrogenic dances.

</details>


### [206] [Counting Beyond Frames: Codec Motion Tells Frozen Diffusion VLMs How Many](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34657)
*Zhuoqin Li,Jiayu Ding,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoDiCount 是一个免训练框架，利用视频编解码器记录的分块位移运动信息构造计数假设检验，并将其作为计数先验注入冻结扩散视觉语言模型的去噪过程以提升视频计数准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 视频计数任务要求模型根据视频回答某事件发生的次数，输入为抽帧视频与计数问题（选项字母或开放整数），输出为正确计数；该任务对自回归与扩散类视频语言模型均极具挑战。

Motivation: 现有模型仅在帧层面采样少数帧，而重复动作发生在帧间，导致计数证据缺失；虽有编解码器稠密运动信息可用，但此前工作仅用它塑造视觉输入，未将其作为计数的直接证据来源。

Method: 论文将候选计数视为可检验的运动假设：通过"展开"计数所隐含的循环结构，度量观测到的运动方向对该假设的支持强度，形成计数势能；在冻结扩散视觉语言模型（dVLM）每一步去噪时，将每个选项字母或语法合法的开放整数编辑映射为对应计数值，生成结构化计数消息，并在该集合内对模型自身概率重新加权，整个过程无需训练。

实验效果: 在 MotionBench、TOMATO 与 SVCBench 上相对官方解码器取得一致提升；7B dVLM 在 MotionBench 上超过 GPT-5.1 与 Gemini-2.5-Pro，在 TOMATO 上超过 GPT-4o 及更大规模开源模型，在 SVCBench 上与 GPT-5.4 持平。

Abstract: Counting how many times something happens is among the simplest questions about a video and among the hardest for video-language models, autoregressive and diffusion alike. The difficulty lies between the frames: a model samples only a few of them, while each repetition unfolds as movement in between. Video codecs already record this movement densely as block displacements, and codec-based video models use it to shape their visual input. We are the first to show that this motion provides direct evidence for the count: a candidate count is a testable motion hypothesis, and unwinding the cycles it implies measures how strongly the observed directions support it. We present CoDiCount, a unified training-free framework that turns this test into a count potential and applies it while a frozen diffusion vision-language model (dVLM) denoises its answer. At every denoising step, structured count messages map each option letter, or each grammar-valid edit of an open integer, to the count it expresses and reweight the model's own probabilities within that set. Consistent gains over the official decoders on MotionBench, TOMATO, and SVCBench demonstrate the broad generalization of CoDiCount across diffusion models, datasets, and diverse counting tasks. With CoDiCount, a 7B dVLM outperforms GPT-5.1 and Gemini-2.5-Pro on MotionBench, surpasses GPT-4o and much larger open models on TOMATO, and matches GPT-5.4 on SVCBench.

</details>


### [207] [Neural Rendering Compiler: Generative Video Models as Programmable Renderers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34680)
*Yuxia Zhu,Xinglong Xu,Hao Liang,Linzhuang Sun,Caijun Jia,Qifeng Cai,Jincen Luo,Lei Li,Alden Cai,Asher Qin,Wentao Zhang,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: The paper proposes NeurCo, a capability-guided "neural rendering compiler" that probes frozen video models to build model-specific capability manuals and compiles model-agnostic user intent into model-specific execution strategies, evaluated on a new 380-task benchmark VComBench.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: The task is to give generative video models a predictable, programmable control interface comparable to HTML/CSS engines or Blender.
